Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geochemistry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 210. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 786 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on geochemistry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 210. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 786. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any geochemistry module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 367. The projective resolution has length at most 800 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the geochemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^367 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 800-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
